Title: Vita del proposto Lodovico Antonio Muratori, già bibliotecario del serenissimo signor Duca di Modena, descritta dal proposto Gian-Francesco Soli Muratori suo nipote; con la giunta in questa edizione napoletana di alcune lettere scritte dallo stesso proposto Lodovico Antonio Muratori a diversi letterati napoletani
Creator: Gian-Francesco Soli Muratori (title page, read on the page image)
Publisher: Naples: presso Giuseppe Ponzelli (title page, read on the page image)
Publication Date: 1758 (title page: 'IN NAPOLI MDCCLVIII', read on the page image, leaf n4, and at OCR line 29; the OCR drops the first title lines)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1758); the archive.org record carries the Public Domain Mark
Language: ita
Source: archive.org item bub_gb_S09fcFzVIu4C, full text from the item's own djvu.txt (bub_gb_S09fcFzVIu4C_djvu.txt). Fetched directly 2026-11-15 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (973,061) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1758, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: readable in the body, with the long s read as f; the first leaves (OCR lines 60-170) are heavily garbled. Google's 'Digitized by' stamp recurs on page breaks, about fifty times. Word count of the host text: 170,531. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: The nephew's Life, with documents. Soli's preface to the reader at about OCR lines 120-235; table of chapters at 246-460. The Vita from 649: chapter II at 1088, III 1508, IV 2258, V 2403, VII 2633, VIII 2972, IX 3791, X 7762, XI 8122, XII about 8292, XIII 8487, XIV 8993, XV 9546, the last chapter at 10333 (the headings of I and VI are garbled). Catalogue of Muratori's works at 11146. Appendix of documents from about 11532, numbered I to XXXIII and beyond: letters to and from Muratori by Marsigli, Zeno, Querini, Mazzocchi and others. Second appendix, a reply to Fontanini, at 16256. Funeral oration at 16613. Index of matters from 17812 to the end. Where the Naples edition's added letters stand was not found.
